{"lang_code":"en","pos":"det","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"How could you eat so much ice-cream?","type":"example"},{"text":"There is only so much information you can remember.","type":"example"},{"text":"I've got so much work to do, but so little time.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A particular amount of (something), often a large or excessive amount."]},{"examples":[{"text":"There isn't so much sand on this beach as there used to be.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A particular amount of (something), often a large or excessive amount.","Making a comparison with another situation; as much."],"raw_tags":["with negatives"],"tags":["usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"There is so much snow, I can't get out of the door.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A particular amount of (something), often a large or excessive amount.","An amount of (something) sufficient to bring about a given consequence."]},{"examples":[{"text":"\"Just so much flour,\" he replied, sprinkling a small pile on the table.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A demonstrated amount of (something)."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈsəʊ ˈmʌtʃ/"}],"upstream_line":1147019,"upstream_offset":4087759748,"upstream_record_sha256":"97270505351c4f3ce1992981ff805f60f27d481bcb32180f56049a133b2f0816","word":"so much"}
{"lang_code":"en","pos":"pron","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"How could you eat so much?","type":"example"},{"text":"There used to be a lot of sand on this beach, but now there isn't so much.","type":"example"},{"text":"Look at all that snow! I've never seen so much!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["As determiner, above, but with the complement omitted and stated elsewhere or understood from context."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Stop spending so much of my money!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["With the complement indicated with of."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈsəʊ ˈmʌtʃ/"}],"upstream_line":1147020,"upstream_offset":4087763725,"upstream_record_sha256":"dded9961139bfc95d8e47aaadb934ffe61860a08ec55c7fb66c9bb762191f093","word":"so much"}
{"lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I love you so much.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To a great degree or extent; very much."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Will you please stop complaining so much!","type":"example"},{"text":"It wasn't so much that I don’t want to, but I just didn't have the time.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To such a degree or extent."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I used to like her a lot, but now I don't like her so much.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To such a degree or extent.","Making a comparison with another situation; as much."],"raw_tags":["with negatives"],"tags":["not-comparable","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Identical twins are so much alike, it is difficult to identify them.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To such a degree or extent.","To a (usually large) extent sufficient to bring about a given consequence."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Don't over-whisk the mixture – only whisk it so much.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To a demonstrated or implied degree or extent; this much, that much."],"tags":["not-comparable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈsəʊ ˈmʌtʃ/"}],"upstream_line":1147021,"upstream_offset":4087767051,"upstream_record_sha256":"2d28fd772d061e07feca1d6263af1e1d999edb49fe2550b5dfab4f036ded9960","word":"so much"}

{"forms":[{"form":"whiche","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"wich","tags":["alternative","Jamaica"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"det","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Which song shall we play?","type":"example"},{"text":"They couldn't decide which song to play.","type":"example"},{"text":"Which one is bigger?","type":"example"},{"text":"Show me which one is bigger.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["What, of those mentioned or implied."],"tags":["interrogative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Take which one you want.","type":"example"},{"text":"You may go which way you please.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The/Any ... that; whichever."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He once owned a painting of the house, which painting would later be stolen.","type":"example"},{"text":"She only speaks Chinese, which language I can hardly follow.","type":"example"},{"text":"For several seconds he sat in silence, during which time the tea and sandwiches arrived.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'm thinking of getting a new car, in which case I'd get a red one.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Designates the one(s) previously mentioned."],"qualifier":"formal outside certain phrases","tags":["relative"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"wĭch"},{"ipa":"/wɪt͡ʃ/"},{"enpr":"hwĭch"},{"ipa":"/ʍɪt͡ʃ/"}],"upstream_line":25215,"upstream_offset":173659326,"upstream_record_sha256":"1510e6b040b1e6be81c92b2e262cc5abf955d148fa892e9c179a04b709d8b765","word":"which"}
{"forms":[{"form":"whiche","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"wich","tags":["alternative","Jamaica"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"pron","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Which is which?","type":"example"},{"text":"By now, you must surely know which is which.","type":"example"},{"text":"Which is bigger, the red one or the blue one?","type":"example"},{"text":"I'm unable to determine which is bigger.","type":"example"},{"text":"Which's your spouse? -The one in trousers.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["What one or ones (of those mentioned or implied)."],"tags":["interrogative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Please take which you please.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The/Any ones that; whichever."],"raw_tags":["not in common use"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I dropped a saucepan, which knocked over the eggs, which went all over the kitchen floor.","type":"example"},{"text":"I found my camera, which I thought I'd lost, under the bed.","type":"example"},{"text":"I entered the room, at the far end of which was a small table.","type":"example"},{"text":"I am enclosing an application form, which I should be grateful if you'd sign and return.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a non-restrictive relative clause, referring to something previously mentioned.","Referring to a preceding noun."],"tags":["relative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She had a young child, which cried incessantly.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a non-restrictive relative clause, referring to something previously mentioned.","Referring to a preceding noun.","Used of people (now generally who, whom, that; which remains possible with words also referred to by it such as baby, child)."],"tags":["archaic","relative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The front door was open, which concerned me.","type":"example"},{"text":"He had to leave, which was very difficult.","type":"example"},{"text":"She saved my life, for which I am eternally grateful.","type":"example"},{"text":"You were late, which was bad, but what's worse, you haven't even apologized yet.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a non-restrictive relative clause, referring to something previously mentioned.","Referring to a preceding statement."],"tags":["relative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"This is the letter which I received.","type":"example"},{"text":"This is the letter in which he explains his decision.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a restrictive relative clause, referring to a noun previously mentioned."],"tags":["proscribed","relative","sometimes"]},{"glosses":["In a restrictive relative clause, referring to a noun previously mentioned.","Used of people."],"tags":["archaic","proscribed","relative","sometimes"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"wĭch"},{"ipa":"/wɪt͡ʃ/"},{"enpr":"hwĭch"},{"ipa":"/ʍɪt͡ʃ/"}],"upstream_line":25216,"upstream_offset":173708956,"upstream_record_sha256":"179f302147fec85ef18683a4382eb1bd07c13b68dff695f0d12969640edd65a2","word":"which"}
